FreeCAD CodeQL Memory Triage

44 clusters covering 215 findings. Source links target FreeCAD/FreeCAD at 0da231857ad1431295f252174cefce441966bffa.

1 moderate severity 36 cluster-0040 (5 findings)

Running mesh operations or inspection that builds downward connectivity for a mesh containing quadratic, hexahedral, wedge, or related volume cell types, then discarding the mesh.

Reproduction: Create or import a volume mesh containing the affected cell types, invoke the mesh API or GUI operation that builds downward connectivity, destroy the mesh without calling CleanDownWardConnectivity, and repeat while monitoring memory. SMDS_UnstructuredGrid destruction leaves the raw _downArray entries allocated.

Recommended action: Fix SMDS_UnstructuredGrid destruction so it releases _downArray, preferably by calling CleanDownwardConnectivity from the destructor or by using an equivalent owning container, while preserving safe cleanup for explicit calls. Add a regression test that builds downward connectivity and destroys the grid.

6 low severity 12 cluster-0029 (10 findings)

Activate the TechDraw workbench and use its workbench-specific toolbars, views, dimensions, or annotations.

Reproduction: Switch to the TechDraw workbench, use its commands, then switch to another workbench or exit FreeCAD. The workbench root owns the created toolbar-manager children and recursively deletes them when the root is destroyed.

Recommended action: No code change. The returned workbench root owns all registered children and recursively releases them.

Render or redraw a TechDraw part view containing edges, faces, vertices, section lines, center lines, or highlights.

Reproduction: Create a TechDraw page with a part view, enable section or center-line decorations, force a redraw by changing the source or view properties, and close the page. Group cleanup removes the generated graphics items and their decorations.

Recommended action: No code change. QGraphicsItem group ownership and the explicit primitive/decorative cleanup paths release the allocations.

Use TechDraw part-view decorations such as matting, break lines, or symbol display areas.

Reproduction: Create a TechDraw page and apply a matting, break-line, or symbol decoration to a view, redraw the view several times, then close the page. The generated items are removed and deleted through their owning graphics groups.

Recommended action: No code change. The graphics-item groups own these children and explicitly delete them during decoration cleanup.

32 low severity 4 cluster-0031 (5 findings)

Using the Robot workbench or Python API to create a Trajectory and call insertWaypoints, position, or deleteLast.

Reproduction: Enable the Robot module, open FreeCAD's Python console, create a Robot.Trajectory with waypoints, repeatedly call insertWaypoints, position, and deleteLast, then delete references and run garbage collection. The returned Python wrappers are reclaimed normally.

Recommended action: Close as a false positive or document the Python wrapper ownership contract. No code fix is needed.
